x ^Qm6IwR4o

This stage focuses on the meaning of the code rather than its syntax. In other words, even if a statement is syntactically correct, it still needs to be semantically valid. ^Ip5WbgDJ

This stage transforms the Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) into an Intermediate Representation (IR). ^JBPQGehU

final stage ^WR165y4Q

Code Optimizer ^nmjh41wp

This stage takes the intermediate code and optimizes it by removing unnecessary instructions and improving its efficiency in terms of execution speed and memory usage. ^LFBqjF5h
